<span class="ct-node-label">Rendlesham</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">The Rendlesham Forest UFO incident is Britain’s best-known UFO case: a cluster of reported unexplained lights and alleged landing traces near RAF Woodbridge in Suffolk over two nights in late December 1980.</span>
</span>
